rule (modulo E) Register:
   [ Fr( ~skc ), Fr( ~skp ) ]
  --[ Reg( $A ), OnlyOnce( $A ), IdentityIs( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) )
  ]->
   [
   !LtkC( $A, ~skc ), !LtkP( $A, ~skp ), !Pk( $A, pk(~skc) ),
   !PkC( $A, pk(~skc) ), !PkP( $A, pk(~skp) ),
   !Kh( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ), Out( <pk(~skc), pk(~skp)> )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_Classical:
   [ !LtkC( $A, ~skc ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedClassical( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skc ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_PostQuantum:
   [ !LtkP( $A, ~skp ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedPQ( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skp ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise_Without_Meeting:
   [
   !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), !Kh( $S, priorKh ),
   In( <claimKh, newkey, result> )
   ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, result ), RecognisedWithoutMeeting( $V )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, claimKh, priorKh, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh, priorKh, newkey, result)>,
              ~vsc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh, priorKh, newkey, result)>, ~vsp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Old_Key_Proof:
   [ !LtkC( $S, ~oldc ), !LtkP( $S, ~oldp ), In( newkey ), !Pk( $P, pkP ) ]
  --[ OldKeyProof( $S, newkey ), OldKeyProofFor( $S, newkey, $P ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~oldc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~oldp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Met:
   [ ] --[ PriorCounterparty( $V, $S ) ]-> [ !Met( $V, $S ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recovery_Meeting:
   [ !Met( $V, $S ), Fr( ~nk ) ]
  --[ RecoveryMeeting( $V, $S ), MeetingKey( $V, $S, ~nk ) ]->
   [ AtMeeting( $V, $S, ~nk ), Out( ~nk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise:
   [
   !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), !Kh( $S, khS ), !Kh( $V, khVself ),
   AtMeeting( $V, $S, newkey )
   ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, 'match' ),
  HonestlyRecognised( $V, $S, newkey )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'recognise', $S, newkey, 'match', khVself, khS,

         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(khVself, khS, newkey, 'match')>, ~vsp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction AtLeastOneMatchInTheBlock:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  */

rule (modulo E) Transfer_Countersign:
   [
   !LtkC( $Old, ~oc ), !LtkP( $Old, ~op ), In( nodekey ), !Pk( $New, pkNew )
   ]
  --[ CountersignedFor( $Old, nodekey, $New ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'transfer', $Old, <nodekey, $Old, $New>, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~oc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~op)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
   [
   !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ), In( adoptNode ),
   In( <'transfer', mapOld, <stmtNode, stmtOld, stmtNew>, provC, provP> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ), Eq( mapOld, $Old ),
  Eq( verify(provC, <'rhtn/1:transfer', stmtNode, stmtOld, stmtNew>,
             fst(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provP, <'rhtn/1:transfer', stmtNode, stmtOld, stmtNew>,
             snd(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( stmtNode, adoptNode ), Eq( stmtOld, mapOld ), Eq( stmtNew, $New ),
  Distinct( $Old, $New ),
  PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, adoptNode )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'adopted', adoptNode, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
     [
     !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ), In( adoptNode ),
     In( <'transfer', mapOld, <stmtNode, stmtOld, stmtNew>, provC, provP> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ), Eq( mapOld, $Old ), Eq( z, true ),
    Eq( z.1, true ), Eq( stmtNode, adoptNode ), Eq( stmtOld, mapOld ),
    Eq( stmtNew, $New ), Distinct( $Old, $New ),
    PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, adoptNode )
    ]->
     [ Out( sign(<'adopted', adoptNode, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. kmOldT
             = kmOldT.24
       provC = provC.26
       provP = provP.27

       stmtNode

                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', stmtNode.29, stmtOld.30, stmtNew.28>, fst(kmOldT.24))
       z.1   = verify(provP.27,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', stmtNode.29, stmtOld.30, stmtNew.28>, snd(kmOldT.24))
    
    2. kmOldT
             = <x.46, x.47>
       provC = provC.28

       stmtNode

       z.1   = verify(provP.29,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', stmtNode.31, stmtOld.32, stmtNew.30>, x.47)
    
    3. kmOldT
             = <x.46, pk(x.47)>
       provC = provC.28

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Recovery:
   [
   !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), !Kh( $V2, khV2 ),
   In( <kmOld, kmV, kmV2> ), In( adoptNew ),
   In( <'rotate', $S, stmtNew, stmtPat, provAc, provAp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, respNew1, result, claimKh, priorKh, provBc, provBp>
   ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, respNew2, result2, claimKh2, priorKh2, provB2c, 
        provB2p>
   )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ), Eq( h(kmV2), khV2 ),
  Eq( respNew2, adoptNew ), Distinct( $V, $V2 ),
  Distinct( khV2, adoptNew ), Eq( claimKh2, khV2 ),
  ResponseNames( $V2, claimKh2 ), Eq( priorKh2, khOld ),
  Eq( verify(provB2c,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh2, priorKh2, respNew2, result2)>,
             fst(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provB2p,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh2, priorKh2, respNew2, result2)>,
             snd(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAc, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, stmtNew, stmtPat)>,
             fst(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAp, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, stmtNew, stmtPat)>,
             snd(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( stmtNew, adoptNew ), Eq( $P, $P ), Distinct( khV, adoptNew ),
  Eq( claimKh, khV ), ResponseNames( $V, claimKh ), Eq( priorKh, khOld ),
  Eq( respNew1, adoptNew ),
  Eq( verify(provBc,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh, priorKh, respNew1, result)>,
             fst(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provBp,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(claimKh, priorKh, respNew1, result)>,
             snd(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  BlockResults( result, result2 ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $V2, $S ),
  Distinct( $P, $S ), Distinct( khOld, adoptNew ),
  ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V, $S, adoptNew, result ),
  ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V2, $S, adoptNew, result2 ),
  ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ), ReliedOn( $P, $V2, $S ),
  PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, adoptNew )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, adoptNew>, ~psk) ) ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Patron_Accept_Recovery:
     [
     !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), !Kh( $V2, khV2 ),
     In( <kmOld, kmV, kmV2> ), In( adoptNew ),
     In( <'rotate', $S, stmtNew, stmtPat, provAc, provAp> ),
     In( <'recognise', $S, respNew1, result, claimKh, priorKh, provBc, provBp>
     ),
     In( <'recognise', $S, respNew2, result2, claimKh2, priorKh2, provB2c, 
          provB2p>
     )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ), Eq( h(kmV2), khV2 ),
    Eq( respNew2, adoptNew ), Distinct( $V, $V2 ),
    Distinct( khV2, adoptNew ), Eq( claimKh2, khV2 ),
    ResponseNames( $V2, claimKh2 ), Eq( priorKh2, khOld ), Eq( z, true ),
    Eq( z.1, true ), Eq( z.2, true ), Eq( z.3, true ),
    Eq( stmtNew, adoptNew ), Eq( $P, $P ), Distinct( khV, adoptNew ),
    Eq( claimKh, khV ), ResponseNames( $V, claimKh ), Eq( priorKh, khOld ),
    Eq( respNew1, adoptNew ), Eq( z.4, true ), Eq( z.5, true ),
    BlockResults( result, result2 ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $V2, $S ),
    Distinct( $P, $S ), Distinct( khOld, adoptNew ),
    ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V, $S, adoptNew, result ),
    ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V2, $S, adoptNew, result2 ),
    ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ), ReliedOn( $P, $V2, $S ),
    PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, adoptNew )
    ]->
     [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, adoptNew>, ~psk) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)
      1. $S    = $S.65
         claimKh
               = claimKh.70
         claimKh2
               = claimKh2.71
         kmOld = kmOld.75
         kmV   = kmV.76
         kmV2  = kmV2.77
         priorKh
               = priorKh.78
         priorKh2
               = priorKh2.79
         provAc
               = provAc.80
         provAp

         provB2p
               = provB2p.83
         provBc
               = provBc.84
         provBp
               = provBp.85
         respNew1
               = respNew1.86
         respNew2
               = respNew2.87
         result
               = result.88
         result2
               = result2.89
         stmtNew
               = stmtNew.90
         stmtPat
               = stmtPat.91
         z     = verify(provB2c.82,
                        <'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                         vresp(claimKh2.71, priorKh2.79, respNew2.87, result2.89)>,
                        fst(kmV2.77))
         z.1   = verify(provB2p.83,
                        <'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                         vresp(claimKh2.71, priorKh2.79, respNew2.87, result2.89)>,
                        snd(kmV2.77))

restriction OneKeyPerName:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  */

restriction VerifierIsNotSubject:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  */

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_both_verifier_halves:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #k. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i))"
*/
by sorry

lemma no_relied_on_verifier_is_the_subject:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey #i.
    ((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
     (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ⇒
    (¬(V = S))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  V = S"
*/
by sorry

lemma no_relied_on_verifier_is_the_new_subject:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey result #i.
    (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i) ⇒
    (¬(∃ #r. IdentityIs( V, newkey ) @ #r))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey result #i.
  (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∃ #r. (IdentityIs( V, newkey ) @ #r)"
*/
by sorry

lemma the_named_verifier_is_the_signing_verifier:
  all-traces
  "∀ V claimKh #i.
    (ResponseNames( V, claimKh ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ #r. (IdentityIs( V, claimKh ) @ #r) ∧ (#r < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ V claimKh #i.
  (ResponseNames( V, claimKh ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∀ #r. (IdentityIs( V, claimKh ) @ #r) ⇒ ¬(#r < #i)"
*/
by sorry

lemma every_relied_on_response_was_signed_as_such:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey result #i.
    (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, result ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey result #i.
  (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, result ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
by sorry

lemma acceptance_requires_a_signed_match:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ V. ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #i)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∀ V. (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #i) ⇒ ⊥"
*/
by sorry

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_the_classical_half_too:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #k. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i))"
*/
by sorry

lemma a_recovery_never_installs_the_prior_identity:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i #r.
    ((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
     (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)) ⇒
    (⊥)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i #r.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)"
*/
by sorry

lemma recovery_completes_honestly:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P S newkey V #m #r #i.
    (((((((MeetingKey( V, S, newkey ) @ #m) ∧

  "∀ P S newkey #i.

     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
